Public home, robots, sitemap, representative nutrition page, trust pages, invalid URL behavior, AdSense loader, and ads.txt were independently verified healthy on 2026-08-30. Search Console reported 2,832 submitted URLs, zero warnings or errors, and `pending=false` before that one automatic submission.
- Completed work: Changed the scheduled GSC workflow from an hourly unconditional sitemap submission with a `pending=false` gate to a daily read-only live/status check. Sitemap submission is now available only through an explicit manual boolean input that defaults to `false`. Added read-only workflow permissions and concurrency control while preserving the repository's existing `npm install` behavior. Removed the duplicate `main`-push GSC submission job from `ci.yml`, added explicit read-only token permission there, and kept normal typecheck/build CI intact.

- Validation evidence: Both YAML files parse and ten combined workflow assertions pass: daily read-only status monitoring, manual submission defaulting false, least-privilege tokens, and CI containing only the normal verify job with no submit command. The real read-only commands `gsc:sitemap:check-live` and `gsc:sitemap:status` passed; the latter confirmed site-owner visibility, 2,832 submitted URLs, zero warnings/errors, and `pending=false`. `npm run typecheck` passed. `npm run build` compiled successfully and generated 415 static pages. Full `npm run lint` remains red only on two pre-existing unescaped-quote errors in `app/page.tsx:172` plus one pre-existing image warning; neither file is part of this change. `npm ci` was also tested and correctly rejected the repository's currently inconsistent lockfile, so workflows retain `npm install`.
